1e:["$","td","1",{"className":"min-w-44 py-3 pr-4 text-sm","children":["$","div",null,{"className":"space-y-1.5","children":[["$","p",null,{"children":[["$","span",null,{"className":"num whitespace-nowrap","children":"$$5.28T"}]," a year, ",["$","span",null,{"className":"num whitespace-nowrap","children":"+$5.5B"}]," against today"]}],["$","p",null,{"className":"text-xs text-muted-foreground","children":["Federal ",["$","span",null,{"className":"num whitespace-nowrap","children":"+$16.6B"}],false,false]}],["$","details",null,{"className":"text-xs","children":[["$","summary",null,{"className":"cursor-pointer text-muted-foreground","children":"Line by line"}],["$","ul",null,{"className":"mt-1 space-y-1.5","children":[["$","li","Care for the newly covered (+$5.9B)",{"children":[["$","span",null,{"className":"font-medium","children":["Care for the newly covered (+$5.9B)",":"]}]," ",["$","span",null,{"className":"text-foreground/80","children":"Enrollment is voluntary, so we use the share of the uninsured that Urban's Reform 2 (bigger subsidies and a mandate) covered: 12.1%: 2.5 of 26.7 million uninsured people. About 6.3 million uninsured undocumented immigrants stay ineligible (50.0% of 12.5 million). 79:["$","li","Federal side of premium changes ($0.0B)",{"children":[["$","span",null,{"className":"font-medium","children":["Federal side of premium changes ($0.0B)",":"]}]," ",["$","span",null,{"className":"text-foreground/80","children":"Private premiums change by −$274.7B for people insured today (+$32.0B of it from new benefits and the copay cap, with their overhead).
